uR=function(ppni,a,b)
 local b8av=a-((0x1a87+b*0x83)%0xfff1);local ZS=ppni.dOCXjl;local C0zKo=ZS[b8av]
 if (not mk4r1(C0zKo,(Kkh[0x197C]))) then return C0zKo end;local dpF=ppni.iL0Hm[b8av];if mk4r1(dpF,(Kkh[0x197C])) then return (Kkh[0x197C]) end

end,
fq=function(ppni,a,b)
 local LxS=a-((0xE8EF*0x3+b*0xc5+0x11)%0xFFF1);local Tbgu=ppni.dOCXjl;local VuI=Tbgu[LxS]

end,
